TL;DR: CLD用于软体游泳机器人频率选择性阻抗整形，通过被动增强弯曲阻抗的耗散分量，改善推力生成时的力-速度相位对齐，显著提升瞬态推进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的快速加速和爆发机动更依赖于推力生成时的力-速度相位对齐，而非精确共振维持。需要一种被动机制来选择性调节阻抗特性，以优化瞬态推进性能。

Method: 采用约束层阻尼（CLD）作为被动机制，选择性增强弯曲阻抗的耗散分量，同时保持存储刚度。通过干阻抗测量表征行为，在约束推进测试中验证CLD对推力和力-运动相位关系的影响，并在无约束游泳试验中评估性能提升。

Result: CLD被动地将阻抗组成向耗散主导转变（随驱动频率增加）。在约束推进测试中增强推力并改变力-运动相位关系；在无约束游泳试验中，峰值加速度提升近5倍，终端速度提升3倍。

Conclusion: 相位导向的被动阻抗调制为改善软体机器人系统的瞬态推进提供了一种简单、无需控制的方法，CLD通过选择性增强耗散阻抗优化了力-速度相位对齐。

Abstract: Rapid acceleration and burst maneuvers in underwater robots depend less on maintaining precise resonance and more on force--velocity phase alignment during thrust generation. In this work, we investigate constrained-layer damping (CLD) as a passive mechanism for frequency-selective impedance shaping in soft robotic swimmers. Unlike conventional stiffness-tuning approaches, CLD selectively amplifies the dissipative component of bending impedance while preserving storage stiffness, passively shifting the impedance composition toward dissipative dominance as actuation frequency increases. We characterize this behavior through dry impedance measurements, demonstrate that CLD enhances thrust and alters force--motion phase relationships across Strouhal numbers in constrained propulsion tests, and validate that passive impedance shaping yields a nearly five-fold increase in peak acceleration and a three-fold increase in terminal velocity in unconstrained swimming trials. These results establish phase-oriented passive impedance modulation as a simple, control-free pathway for improving transient propulsion in soft robotic systems.

</details>


### [112] [Real-time loosely coupled GNSS and IMU integration via Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03546)
*Radu-Andrei Cioaca,Cristian Rusu,Paul Irofti,Gianluca Caparra,Andrei-Alexandru Marinache,Florin Stoican*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因子图优化的松耦合GNSS/IMU融合架构，在挑战性城市峡谷环境中实现实时操作并提高服务可用性，但以定位精度为代价


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的定位、导航和定时(PNT)是现代技术运行的基础，也是自主系统的关键使能器。GNSS作为PNT的重要组成部分确保室外定位，但现有研究主要关注GNSS与IMU的融合性能提升，而因子图优化方法通常计算量大且多用于后处理，需要评估其在挑战性环境中的实时性能

Method: 采用松耦合架构将GNSS和IMU测量集成到因子图优化框架中，重点关注在挑战性环境（城市峡谷）中的实时操作能力，并与批处理FGO方法进行对比

Result: 在UrbanNav-HK-MediumUrban-1数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法实现了实时操作，相比批处理FGO方法提高了服务可用性，但这种改进以定位精度降低为代价

Conclusion: 论文详细分析了实时FGO基GNSS/IMU融合中精度、可用性和计算效率之间的权衡关系，为实际应用提供了重要参考

Abstract: Accurate positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT) is fundamental to the operation of modern technologies and a key enabler of autonomous systems. A very important component of PNT is the Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) which ensures outdoor positioning. Modern research directions have pushed the performance of GNSS localization to new heights by fusing GNSS measurements with other sensory information, mainly measurements from Inertial Measurement Units (IMU). In this paper, we propose a loosely coupled architecture to integrate GNSS and IMU measurements using a Factor Graph Optimization (FGO) framework. Because the FGO method can be computationally challenging and often used as a post-processing method, our focus is on assessing its localization accuracy and service availability while operating in real-time in challenging environments (urban canyons). Experimental results on the UrbanNav-HK-MediumUrban-1 dataset show that the proposed approach achieves real-time operation and increased service availability compared to batch FGO methods. While this improvement comes at the cost of reduced positioning accuracy, the paper provides a detailed analysis of the trade-offs between accuracy, availability, and computational efficiency that characterize real-time FGO-based GNSS/IMU fusion.

</details>


### [113] [Real-time tightly coupled GNSS and IMU integration via Factor Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03556)
*Radu-Andrei Cioaca,Paul Irofti,Cristian Rusu,Gianluca Caparra,Andrei-Alexandru Marinache,Florin Stoican*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时紧耦合GNSS-IMU因子图优化方法，通过增量优化和固定滞后边缘化实现因果状态估计，并在高度城市化的GNSS退化环境中评估性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集城市环境中GNSS信号频繁遮挡、多径效应和卫星几何快速变化导致可靠定位困难。虽然基于因子图优化的GNSS-IMU融合已展现出强鲁棒性和精度，但大多数方法仍为离线处理

Method: 实时紧耦合GNSS-IMU因子图优化方法，采用增量优化和固定滞后边缘化实现因果状态估计

Result: 使用UrbanNav数据集在高度城市化的GNSS退化环境中评估性能

Conclusion: 该方法实现了实时GNSS-IMU融合定位，解决了密集城市环境中GNSS信号退化的问题

Abstract: Reliable positioning in dense urban environments remains challenging due to frequent GNSS signal blockage, multipath, and rapidly varying satellite geometry. While factor graph optimization (FGO)-based GNSS-IMU fusion has demonstrated strong robustness and accuracy, most formulations remain offline. In this work, we present a real-time tightly coupled GNSS-IMU FGO method that enables causal state estimation via incremental optimization with fixed-lag marginalization, and we evaluate its performance in a highly urbanized GNSS-degraded environment using the UrbanNav dataset.

</details>


### [114] [MEM: Multi-Scale Embodied Memory for Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03596)
*Marcel Torne,Karl Pertsch,Homer Walke,Kyle Vedder,Suraj Nair,Brian Ichter,Allen Z. Ren,Haohuan Wang,Jiaming Tang,Kyle Stachowicz,Karan Dhabalia,Michael Equi,Quan Vuong,Jost Tobias Springenberg,Sergey Levine,Chelsea Finn,Danny Driess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEM提出了一种多尺度记忆架构，结合视频编码的短期记忆和文本的长期记忆，使机器人策略能够处理长达15分钟的复杂多阶段任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人学习中的记忆通常只是将过去观察序列输入策略，但在复杂的多阶段现实任务中，机器人需要不同粒度的记忆：长期记忆捕捉抽象语义概念（如食谱完成阶段），短期记忆捕捉近期事件并补偿遮挡（如手臂遮挡时记住要拾取的物体）。

Method: 提出多尺度具身记忆（MEM），结合两种模态的记忆：基于视频的短期记忆（通过视频编码器压缩）和基于文本的长期记忆，形成混合模态的长时程记忆架构。

Result: MEM使机器人策略能够执行长达15分钟的任务，如清理厨房或制作烤奶酪三明治。记忆还使MEM策略能够在上下文中智能地适应操作策略。

Conclusion: 有效的机器人记忆架构应该结合多种模态来捕捉不同抽象层次，MEM通过混合视频和文本记忆实现了这一目标，使机器人能够处理复杂的长时程任务。

Abstract: Conventionally, memory in end-to-end robotic learning involves inputting a sequence of past observations into the learned policy. However, in complex multi-stage real-world tasks, the robot's memory must represent past events at multiple levels of granularity: from long-term memory that captures abstracted semantic concepts (e.g., a robot cooking dinner should remember which stages of the recipe are already done) to short-term memory that captures recent events and compensates for occlusions (e.g., a robot remembering the object it wants to pick up once its arm occludes it). In this work, our main insight is that an effective memory architecture for long-horizon robotic control should combine multiple modalities to capture these different levels of abstraction. We introduce Multi-Scale Embodied Memory (MEM), an approach for mixed-modal long-horizon memory in robot policies. MEM combines video-based short-horizon memory, compressed via a video encoder, with text-based long-horizon memory. Together, they enable robot policies to perform tasks that span up to fifteen minutes, like cleaning up a kitchen, or preparing a grilled cheese sandwich. Additionally, we find that memory enables MEM policies to intelligently adapt manipulation strategies in-context.

</details>


### [115] [Touch2Insert: Zero-Shot Peg Insertion by Touching Intersections of Peg and Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03627)
*Masaru Yajima,Yuma Shin,Rei Kawakami,Asako Kanezaki,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Insert：基于触觉感知的零样本任意形状插接框架，通过触觉图像重建截面几何并估计相对位姿，实现无需任务特定训练的工业连接器插接


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业连接器插接是机器人领域的关键挑战，需要亚毫米级精度且常存在视觉遮挡。现有视觉方法受遮挡限制，学习策略难以泛化到未见几何形状。触觉感知能捕获接触点局部几何信息，在遮挡和新型连接器形状下仍能提供可靠信息

Method: 提出Touch2Insert触觉框架：1）从高分辨率触觉图像重建截面几何；2）以零样本方式估计孔相对于插针的相对位姿；3）通过配准对齐重建形状。该方法仅需单次接触，无需任务特定训练

Result: 仿真实验中，所有连接器均实现亚毫米级位姿估计精度；真实机器人实验中，平均成功率86.7%，验证了触觉感知在真实世界连接器插接中的鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: 触觉感知为工业连接器插接提供了可靠解决方案，Touch2Insert框架通过零样本几何重建和位姿估计，在遮挡和未见形状情况下实现高精度插接，展示了触觉感知在机器人操作任务中的实际应用价值

Abstract: Reliable insertion of industrial connectors remains a central challenge in robotics, requiring sub-millimeter precision under uncertainty and often without full visual access. Vision-based approaches struggle with occlusion and limited generalization, while learning-based policies frequently fail to transfer to unseen geometries. To address these limitations, we leverage tactile sensing, which captures local surface geometry at the point of contact and thus provides reliable information even under occlusion and across novel connector shapes. Building on this capability, we present \emph{Touch2Insert}, a tactile-based framework for arbitrary peg insertion. Our method reconstructs cross-sectional geometry from high-resolution tactile images and estimates the relative pose of the hole with respect to the peg in a zero-shot manner. By aligning reconstructed shapes through registration, the framework enables insertion from a single contact without task-specific training. To evaluate its performance, we conducted experiments with three diverse connectors in both simulation and real-robot settings. The results indicate that Touch2Insert achieved sub-millimeter pose estimation accuracy for all connectors in simulation, and attained an average success rate of 86.7\% on the real robot, thereby confirming the robustness and generalizability of tactile sensing for real-world robotic connector insertion.

</details>


### [116] [TreeLoc++: Robust 6-DoF LiDAR Localization in Forests with a Compact Digital Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03695)
*Minwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Nived Chebrolu,Haedam Oh,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TreeLoc++：基于数字森林清单的全局定位框架，无需原始点云数据，在森林环境中实现厘米级精确定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续森林管理需要可靠的定位技术来长期监测树木状态。当前定位方法依赖密集点云数据，存储和维护成本高，而数字森林清单（DFIs）作为森林管理的核心数据结构却被定位研究忽视。

Method: TreeLoc++直接使用DFIs作为判别性表示，通过成对距离直方图编码局部树木布局上下文，结合DBH过滤和偏航一致性内点选择减少误匹配，利用约束优化联合估计滚转、俯仰和高度。

Result: 在3个数据集、4个国家、27个序列上评估，实现厘米级精度定位。仅用250KB地图数据表示15个会话、7.98公里轨迹，优于依赖点云地图的手工和基于学习的基线方法。在2025年数据与2023年清单的两年间隔中表现出长期变化鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TreeLoc++证明了直接使用数字森林清单进行定位的可行性，消除了对密集点云的依赖，提高了定位可靠性，为长期部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable localization is essential for sustainable forest management, as it allows robots or sensor systems to revisit and monitor the status of individual trees over long periods. In modern forestry, this management is structured around Digital Forest Inventories (DFIs), which encode stems using compact geometric attributes rather than raw data. Despite their central role, DFIs have been overlooked in localization research, and most methods still rely on dense gigabyte-sized point clouds that are costly to store and maintain. To improve upon this, we propose TreeLoc++, a global localization framework that operates directly on DFIs as a discriminative representation, eliminating the need to use the raw point clouds. TreeLoc++ reduces false matches in structurally ambiguous forests and improves the reliability of full 6-DoF pose estimation. It augments coarse retrieval with a pairwise distance histogram that encodes local tree-layout context, subsequently refining candidates via DBH-based filtering and yaw-consistent inlier selection to further reduce mismatches. Furthermore, a constrained optimization leveraging tree geometry jointly estimates roll, pitch, and height, enhancing pose stability and enabling accurate localization without reliance on dense 3D point cloud data. Evaluations on 27 sequences recorded in forests across three datasets and four countries show that TreeLoc++ achieves precise localization with centimeter-level accuracy. We further demonstrate robustness to long-term change by localizing data recorded in 2025 against inventories built from 2023 data, spanning a two-year interval. The system represents 15 sessions spanning 7.98 km of trajectories using only 250KB of map data and outperforms both hand-crafted and learning-based baselines that rely on point cloud maps. This demonstrates the scalability of TreeLoc++ for long-term deployment.

</details>


### [117] [UrbanHuRo: A Two-Layer Human-Robot Collaboration Framework for the Joint Optimization of Heterogeneous Urban Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03701)
*Tonmoy Dey,Lin Jiang,Zheng Dong,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UrbanHuRo：一个用于异构城市服务联合优化的两层人机协作框架，通过众包配送和城市感知进行演示，显著提升感知覆盖率和配送员收入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智慧城市研究大多孤立优化单一服务，未充分考虑异构城市服务间的互惠交互可能带来的更高效率和资源利用率提升。例如，人类配送员可在配送路线上收集交通和空气质量数据，而感知机器人可在高峰时段协助按需配送，从而同时提升感知覆盖和配送效率。

Method: 提出UrbanHuRo两层人机协作框架，包含两个关键设计：(1) 基于MapReduce的可扩展分布式K-子模最大化模块，用于高效订单调度；(2) 用于感知路线规划的深度子模奖励强化学习算法。

Result: 在真实世界外卖平台数据集上的实验评估表明，UrbanHuRo在大多数设置下平均提升感知覆盖率29.7%，配送员收入39.2%，同时显著减少超时订单数量。

Conclusion: UrbanHuRo框架成功解决了异构城市服务联合优化的挑战，通过人机协作实现了感知覆盖和配送效率的双重提升，为智慧城市服务协同优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In the vision of smart cities, technologies are being developed to enhance the efficiency of urban services and improve residents' quality of life. However, most existing research focuses on optimizing individual services in isolation, without adequately considering reciprocal interactions among heterogeneous urban services that could yield higher efficiency and improved resource utilization. For example, human couriers could collect traffic and air quality data along their delivery routes, while sensing robots could assist with on-demand delivery during peak hours, enhancing both sensing coverage and delivery efficiency. However, the joint optimization of different urban services is challenging due to potentially conflicting objectives and the need for real-time coordination in dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose UrbanHuRo, a two-layer human-robot collaboration framework for joint optimization of heterogeneous urban services, demonstrated through crowdsourced delivery and urban sensing. UrbanHuRo includes two key designs: (i) a scalable distributed MapReduce-based K-submodular maximization module for efficient order dispatch, and (ii) a deep submodular reward reinforcement learning algorithm for sensing route planning. Experimental evaluations on real-world datasets from a food delivery platform demonstrate that UrbanHuRo improves sensing coverage by 29.7% and courier income by 39.2% on average in most settings, while also significantly reducing the number of overdue orders.

</details>


### [118] [X-Loco: Towards Generalist Humanoid Locomotion Control via Synergetic Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03733)
*Dewei Wang,Xinmiao Wang,Chenyun Zhang,Jiyuan Shi,Yingnan Zhao,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Loco是一个训练视觉通用人形机器人运动策略的框架，通过专家策略协同蒸馏和自适应专家选择机制，实现直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复等多种技能的集成学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人形机器人技能学习面临挑战：虽然单个技能（如直立行走、跌倒恢复、全身协调）已有显著进展，但学习一个能掌握所有这些技能的单一策略仍然困难，因为涉及多样化的动力学和相互冲突的控制目标。

Method: X-Loco框架训练多个专家策略，采用协同策略蒸馏和案例自适应专家选择机制，动态利用多个专家策略来指导基于视觉的学生策略。该设计使学生能够获取广泛的运动技能，包括跌倒恢复、地形穿越和全身协调技能。

Result: X-Loco是首个展示基于视觉的人形机器人运动框架，能够联合集成直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复，仅依赖速度命令而不需要参考运动。实验结果显示X-Loco在跌倒恢复和地形穿越等任务上表现出优越性能。消融研究进一步表明该框架有效利用了专家知识并提高了学习效率。

Conclusion: X-Loco通过专家策略协同蒸馏和自适应选择机制，成功解决了人形机器人多种运动技能集成学习的挑战，实现了首个完全基于视觉、不依赖参考运动的人形机器人通用运动策略框架。

Abstract: While recent advances have demonstrated strong performance in individual humanoid skills such as upright locomotion, fall recovery and whole-body coordination, learning a single policy that masters all these skills remains challenging due to the diverse dynamics and conflicting control objectives involved. To address this, we introduce X-Loco, a framework for training a vision-based generalist humanoid locomotion policy. X-Loco trains multiple oracle specialist policies and adopts a synergetic policy distillation with a case-adaptive specialist selection mechanism, which dynamically leverages multiple specialist policies to guide a vision-based student policy. This design enables the student to acquire a broad spectrum of locomotion skills, ranging from fall recovery to terrain traversal and whole-body coordination skills. To the best of our knowledge, X-Loco is the first framework to demonstrate vision-based humanoid locomotion that jointly integrates upright locomotion, whole-body coordination and fall recovery, while operating solely under velocity commands without relying on reference motions. Experimental results show that X-Loco achieves superior performance, demonstrated by tasks such as fall recovery and terrain traversal. Ablation studies further highlight that our framework effectively leverages specialist expertise and enhances learning efficiency.

</details>


### [119] [Characterization and Correlation of Robotic Snake Scale Friction and Locomotion Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03735)
*Umit Sen,Andri Mahegan,Gina Olson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了新型模块化仿生蛇皮设计，测量了不同攻角鳞片在多种表面的摩擦特性与运动速度，发现摩擦比与运动速度之间缺乏一致相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物蛇通过无腿运动策略和摩擦各向异性皮肤实现多环境运动，但工程系统中如何设计类似摩擦各向异性以及摩擦比对运动性能的影响尚不明确，特别是横向摩擦常被忽视。

Method: 1) 开发模块化、易构建、可更换鳞片的关节式伪皮肤设计；2) 在草皮、树皮、光滑表面、地毯四种表面上，测量15°、25°、35°、45°攻角的鳞片摩擦特性；3) 分别测量各攻角和表面组合下的运动速度。

Result: 观察到摩擦系数与鳞片角度之间存在一致趋势，符合文献和直觉，但未能一致识别摩擦比与运动速度之间的预期相关性。

Conclusion: 要么摩擦比本身不足以预测蛇形机器人的运动速度，要么需要特定的测量方法来准确捕捉这些比值。

Abstract: Snake robots are inspired by the ability of biological snakes to move over rock, grass, leaves, soil, up trees, along pavement and more. Their ability to move in multiple distinct environments is due to their legless locomotion strategy, which combines distinct gaits with a skin that exhibits frictional anisotropy. Designing soft robotic snakes with similar capabilities requires an understanding of how this underlying frictional anisotropy should be created in engineered systems, and how variances in the frictional anisotropy ratio affect locomotion speed and direction on different surfaces. While forward and backward frictional ratios have been characterized for previous scale designs, lateral friction and the associated ratios are often overlooked. In this paper, our contributions include: (i) the development of a novel articulated pseudo-skin design that is modular, easy to construct and has removable or replaceable scales; (ii) experimental measurement of the frictional characteristics of otherwise-identical scales at varying angles of attack (15°, 25°, 35°, 45°) on different surfaces of interest (grass, bark, smooth surface, carpet);(iii) separate measurements of locomotion speed for each angle and surface. Consequently, while we observed some consistent trends between frictional coefficients and scale angle, aligning with literature and intuition, we were not able to consistently identify expected correlations between frictional ratios and locomotion speed. We conclude that either frictional ratios alone are not sufficient to predict the observed speed of a snake robot, or that specific measurement approaches are required to accurately capture these ratios.

</details>


### [120] [Whole-Body Safe Control of Robotic Systems with Koopman Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03740)
*Sebin Jung,Abulikemu Abuduweili,Jiaxing Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的框架，通过学习Koopman嵌入和算子将非线性系统线性化，并与安全集算法(SSA)结合，在单个二次规划(QP)中同时解决跟踪和安全约束，无需单独的安全滤波器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制具有强非线性、高维动力学的机器人仍然具有挑战性，因为带有安全约束的直接非线性优化通常无法实时求解。Koopman算子提供了一种在提升空间中线性表示非线性系统的方法，从而能够使用高效的线性控制。

Method: 提出数据驱动框架：1) 从数据中学习Koopman嵌入和算子；2) 将得到的线性模型与安全集算法(SSA)集成；3) 在单个二次规划(QP)中同时求解跟踪和安全约束，确保可行性和最优性，无需单独的安全滤波器。

Result: 在Kinova Gen3机械臂和Go2四足机器人上验证了该方法，展示了准确的跟踪性能和避障能力。

Conclusion: 通过结合Koopman线性化表示和安全集算法，实现了对强非线性、高维机器人系统的实时安全控制，将跟踪和安全约束统一在单个二次规划中求解，提高了控制效率。

Abstract: Controlling robots with strongly nonlinear, high-dimensional dynamics remains challenging, as direct nonlinear optimization with safety constraints is often intractable in real time. The Koopman operator offers a way to represent nonlinear systems linearly in a lifted space, enabling the use of efficient linear control. We propose a data-driven framework that learns a Koopman embedding and operator from data, and integrates the resulting linear model with the Safe Set Algorithm (SSA). This allows the tracking and safety constraints to be solved in a single quadratic program (QP), ensuring feasibility and optimality without a separate safety filter. We validate the method on a Kinova Gen3 manipulator and a Go2 quadruped, showing accurate tracking and obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [121] [HALyPO: Heterogeneous-Agent Lyapunov Policy Optimization for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03741)
*Hao Zhang,Yaru Niu,Yikai Wang,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALyPO：一种异构智能体李雅普诺夫策略优化方法，通过参数空间李雅普诺夫稳定性认证来稳定去中心化策略学习，解决人机协作中的理性差距问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中人类行为的组合多样性和上下文变化要求机器人具备更好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。机器人-人类之间的异构性导致学习过程中出现理性差距，即去中心化最佳响应动态与集中式合作上升之间的变分不匹配，使得独立策略梯度更新可能振荡或发散。

Method: 提出异构智能体李雅普诺夫策略优化（HALyPO），在策略参数空间中直接建立形式稳定性，通过对参数空间分歧度量施加每步李雅普诺夫递减条件。通过最优二次投影修正去中心化梯度，确保理性差距的单调收缩，实现开放交互空间的有效探索。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界人形机器人实验表明，这种认证稳定性在协作边界情况下提高了泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HALyPO通过李雅普诺夫稳定性认证稳定去中心化策略学习，有效解决了异构智能体强化学习中的理性差距问题，为人机协作提供了更可靠的学习框架。

Abstract: To improve generalization and resilience in human-robot collaboration (HRC), robots must handle the combinatorial diversity of human behaviors and contexts, motivating multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). However, inherent heterogeneity between robots and humans creates a rationality gap (RG) in the learning process-a variational mismatch between decentralized best-response dynamics and centralized cooperative ascent. The resulting learning problem is a general-sum differentiable game, so independent policy-gradient updates can oscillate or diverge without added structure. We propose heterogeneous-agent Lyapunov policy optimization (HALyPO), which establishes formal stability directly in the policy-parameter space by enforcing a per-step Lyapunov decrease condition on a parameter-space disagreement metric. Unlike Lyapunov-based safe RL, which targets state/trajectory constraints in constrained Markov decision processes, HALyPO uses Lyapunov certification to stabilize decentralized policy learning. HALyPO rectifies decentralized gradients via optimal quadratic projections, ensuring monotonic contraction of RG and enabling effective exploration of open-ended interaction spaces. Extensive simulations and real-world humanoid-robot experiments show that this certified stability improves generalization and robustness in collaborative corner cases.

</details>


### [122] [Interaction-Aware Whole-Body Control for Compliant Object Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03751)
*Hao Zhang,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种生物启发的交互导向全身控制框架，通过分离上下半身控制、结合轨迹优化和强化学习，实现机器人在紧密接触任务中的稳定物体运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的协作物体运输对辅助人形机器人具有挑战性，因为强时变交互力会使基于跟踪的全身控制不可靠，特别是在紧密接触支持任务中。需要一种能够处理复杂交互力同时保持平衡的控制方法。

Method: 提出交互导向全身控制框架，结构上分离上半身交互执行和下半身支撑控制。使用轨迹优化参考生成器提供运动学先验，强化学习策略管理重载交互和扰动下的身体响应。通过非对称师生蒸馏部署策略，运行时仅依赖本体感知历史。

Result: 广泛实验表明，IO-WBC在精确速度跟踪不可行时仍能保持稳定的全身行为和物理交互，实现了跨多种场景的顺应性物体运输。策略在随机化有效载荷质量/惯性和外部扰动的仿真中训练，并成功部署到实际机器人。

Conclusion: IO-WBC框架作为人工小脑，能够将上游技能级命令转换为稳定、物理一致的全身行为，在紧密耦合的机器人-物体系统中保持平衡并塑造力交换，为紧密接触支持任务提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Cooperative object transport in unstructured environments remains challenging for assistive humanoids because strong, time-varying interaction forces can make tracking-centric whole-body control unreliable, especially in close-contact support tasks. This paper proposes a bio-inspired, interaction-oriented whole-body control (IO-WBC) that functions as an artificial cerebellum - an adaptive motor agent that translates upstream (skill-level) commands into stable, physically consistent whole-body behavior under contact. This work structurally separates upper-body interaction execution from lower-body support control, enabling the robot to maintain balance while shaping force exchange in a tightly coupled robot-object system. A trajectory-optimized reference generator (RG) provides a kinematic prior, while a reinforcement learning (RL) policy governs body responses under heavy-load interactions and disturbances. The policy is trained in simulation with randomized payload mass/inertia and external perturbations, and deployed via asymmetric teacher-student distillation so that the student relies only on proprioceptive histories at runtime. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IO-WBC maintains stable whole-body behavior and physical interaction even when precise velocity tracking becomes infeasible, enabling compliant object transport across a wide range of scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [Cognition to Control - Multi-Agent Learning for Human-Humanoid Collaborative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03768)
*Hao Zhang,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出C2C三层架构，将认知推理与连续控制分离，通过VLM基础层、基于MARL的协调层和全身控制层实现人机协作中的长期协调与稳定接触控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作系统主要关注反应式行为，缺乏将系统2式持续推理与低延迟连续控制整合的机制，这在需要长期协调和物理接触的人机协作中尤为关键

Method: 提出认知到控制三层架构：1) VLM基础层维护场景参照物并推断具身感知的可用性/约束；2) 协调层作为系统2核心，通过基于马尔可夫势博弈的分散式MARL优化长期技能选择和序列；3) 全身控制层高频执行技能并保证运动学/动力学可行性和接触稳定性

Result: 在协作操作任务中相比单智能体和端到端基线方法表现出更高的成功率和鲁棒性，实现了稳定协调和涌现的领导者-跟随者行为

Conclusion: C2C架构通过显式的推理到控制路径，有效整合了长期协调决策与实时物理控制，解决了人机协作中持续推理与连续控制的整合难题

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration (HRC) requires translating high-level intent into contact-stable whole-body motion while continuously adapting to a human partner. Many vision-language-action (VLA) systems learn end-to-end mappings from observations and instructions to actions, but they often emphasize reactive (System 1-like) behavior and leave under-specified how sustained System 2-style deliberation can be integrated with reliable, low-latency continuous control. This gap is acute in multi-agent HRC, where long-horizon coordination decisions and physical execution must co-evolve under contact, feasibility, and safety constraints. We address this limitation with cognition-to-control (C2C), a three-layer hierarchy that makes the deliberation-to-control pathway explicit: (i) a VLM-based grounding layer that maintains persistent scene referents and infers embodiment-aware affordances/constraints; (ii) a deliberative skill/coordination layer-the System 2 core-that optimizes long-horizon skill choices and sequences under human-robot coupling via decentralized MARL cast as a Markov potential game with a shared potential encoding task progress; and (iii) a whole-body control layer that executes the selected skills at high frequency while enforcing kinematic/dynamic feasibility and contact stability. The deliberative layer is realized as a residual policy relative to a nominal controller, internalizing partner dynamics without explicit role assignment. Experiments on collaborative manipulation tasks show higher success and robustness than single-agent and end-to-end baselines, with stable coordination and emergent leader-follower behaviors.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Surgical Robotic Manipulation with 3D Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03798)
*Yu Sheng,Lidian Wang,Xiaomeng Chu,Jiajun Deng,Min Cheng,Yanyong Zhang,Bei Hua,Houqiang Li,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SST是一种端到端视觉运动策略，通过直接探索内窥镜图像中的3D空间线索，赋予手术机器人3D空间感知能力，无需显式重建场景或额外手腕摄像头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术机器人3D感知方法存在明显局限：显式重建方法容易导致误差累积且无法端到端优化；添加手腕摄像头的方法在临床实践中很少采用，因为会干扰机器人手臂运动。需要一种更实用、端到端的解决方案。

Method: 1. 构建Surgical3D大规模逼真数据集（3万对立体内窥镜图像，含精确3D几何）；2. 微调几何transformer从立体内窥镜图像提取鲁棒3D潜在表示；3. 通过轻量级多层次空间特征连接器（MSFC）将这些表示与机器人动作空间对齐，全部在内窥镜中心坐标系中完成。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，SST在复杂手术任务（如打结和离体器官解剖）上达到最先进性能，并展现出强大的空间泛化能力，向实际临床部署迈出重要一步。

Conclusion: SST通过端到端学习内窥镜图像中的3D空间线索，解决了现有方法的局限性，为手术机器人提供了实用的3D空间感知能力，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Achieving 3D spatial awareness is crucial for surgical robotic manipulation, where precise and delicate operations are required. Existing methods either explicitly reconstruct the surgical scene prior to manipulation, or enhance multi-view features by adding wrist-mounted cameras to supplement the default stereo endoscopes. However, both paradigms suffer from notable limitations: the former easily leads to error accumulation and prevents end-to-end optimization due to its multi-stage nature, while the latter is rarely adopted in clinical practice since wrist-mounted cameras can interfere with the motion of surgical robot arms. In this work, we introduce the Spatial Surgical Transformer (SST), an end-to-end visuomotor policy that empowers surgical robots with 3D spatial awareness by directly exploring 3D spatial cues embedded in endoscopic images. First, we build Surgical3D, a large-scale photorealistic dataset containing 30K stereo endoscopic image pairs with accurate 3D geometry, addressing the scarcity of 3D data in surgical scenes. Based on Surgical3D, we finetune a powerful geometric transformer to extract robust 3D latent representations from stereo endoscopes images. These representations are then seamlessly aligned with the robot's action space via a lightweight multi-level spatial feature connector (MSFC), all within an endoscope-centric coordinate frame. Extensive real-robot experiments demonstrate that SST achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong spatial generalization on complex surgical tasks such as knot tying and ex-vivo organ dissection, representing a significant step toward practical clinical deployment. The dataset and code will be released.

</details>


### [125] [SkillVLA: Tackling Combinatorial Diversity in Dual-Arm Manipulation via Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03836)
*Xuanran Zhai,Zekai Huang,Longyan Wu,Qianyou Zhao,Qiaojun Yu,Jieji Ren,Ce Hao,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkillVLA框架通过显式支持技能重用解决双臂操作中的组合多样性问题，将成功率从0%提升到51%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂视觉-语言-动作模型忽略了组合多样性挑战，不同单臂行为配对会产生不同任务行为，但现有模型未显式考虑这种结构。有效双臂VLA应支持技能重用，避免为每个可能组合单独学习，但当前设计使技能在双臂间纠缠，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出SkillVLA框架，专门设计用于在双臂操作中实现技能重用。该框架显式支持将先前学习的单臂技能重新组合到新的左右配对中，避免技能在双臂间纠缠。

Result: SkillVLA显著改善了技能组合能力，将整体成功率从0%提升到51%，并在协作和长时域任务上表现出强大性能。

Conclusion: SkillVLA通过显式支持技能重用解决了双臂操作中的组合多样性问题，提高了模型的可扩展性和性能，为复杂双臂操作任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in vision-language-action (VLA) models has demonstrated strong potential for dual-arm manipulation, enabling complex behaviors and generalization to unseen environments. However, mainstream bimanual VLA formulations largely overlook the critical challenge of combinatorial diversity. Different pairings of single-arm behaviors can induce qualitatively distinct task behaviors, yet existing models do not explicitly account for this structure. We argue that effective bimanual VLAs should support skill reuse - the ability to recombine previously learned single-arm skills across novel left-right pairings - thereby avoiding the need to separately learn every possible combination. Current VLA designs entangle skills across arms, preventing such recomposition and limiting scalability. To address this limitation, we propose SkillVLA, a framework explicitly designed to enable skill reuse in dual-arm manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SkillVLA substantially improves skill composition, increasing overall success rate from 0% to 51%, and achieves strong performance on cooperative and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [126] [IROSA: Interactive Robot Skill Adaptation using Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03897)
*Markus Knauer,Samuel Bustamante,Thomas Eiband,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Freek Stulp,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于工具架构的开放词汇技能适应框架，利用预训练LLM选择和参数化工具来调整机器人技能，无需微调或直接模型-机器人交互，在工业轴承环插入任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在多个领域展现出强大能力，模仿学习为从有限数据中适应机器人技能提供了原则性方法。结合这两种方法对机器人直接应用具有巨大潜力，但这一结合在工业部署中关注有限。

Method: 提出基于工具的架构框架，在语言模型和机器人硬件之间保持保护性抽象层。利用预训练LLM选择和参数化特定工具来适应机器人技能，无需微调或直接模型-机器人交互。

Result: 在7自由度扭矩控制机器人上演示了工业轴承环插入任务，通过自然语言命令成功实现技能适应，包括速度调整、轨迹修正和障碍物避让，同时保持安全性、透明度和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业机器人部署提供了一种安全、透明、可解释的开放词汇技能适应方法，成功结合了基础模型和模仿学习的优势，无需直接模型-机器人交互。

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated impressive capabilities across diverse domains, while imitation learning provides principled methods for robot skill adaptation from limited data. Combining these approaches holds significant promise for direct application to robotics, yet this combination has received limited attention, particularly for industrial deployment. We present a novel framework that enables open-vocabulary skill adaptation through a tool-based architecture, maintaining a protective abstraction layer between the language model and robot hardware. Our approach leverages pre-trained LLMs to select and parameterize specific tools for adapting robot skills without requiring fine-tuning or direct model-to-robot interaction. We demonstrate the framework on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot performing an industrial bearing ring insertion task, showing successful skill adaptation through natural language commands for speed adjustment, trajectory correction, and obstacle avoidance while maintaining safety, transparency, and interpretability.

</details>


### [127] [Lightweight Visual Reasoning for Socially-Aware Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03942)
*Alessio Galatolo,Ronald Cumbal,Alexandros Rouchitsas,Katie Winkle,Didem Gürdür Broo,Ginevra Castellano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级语言到视觉反馈模块，通过门控MLP将图像token隐藏状态投影回视觉编码器输入，在文本上下文下进行二次推理，提升VLM在机器人任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的机器人系统在应对多模态人机交互复杂性方面存在局限，需要提升机器人对动态、不可预测人类行为的理解和响应能力。

Method: 设计轻量级语言到视觉反馈模块，通过门控MLP将图像token隐藏状态投影回视觉编码器输入，实现基于文本上下文的场景二次推理。

Result: 在三个机器人任务中取得显著提升：Qwen 2.5 (7B)在导航任务中减少3.3%距离，场景描述得分提升0.057，意图识别准确率提升2.93%；Gemma 3和LLaVA OV 1.5在后两个任务中也有明显改进。

Conclusion: 提出的反馈模块能有效提升VLM在机器人任务中的推理能力，仅需不到3%额外参数，为多模态人机交互提供了轻量高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in shared human environments must not only navigate, interact, and detect their surroundings, they must also interpret and respond to dynamic, and often unpredictable, human behaviours. Although recent advances have shown promise in enhancing robotic perception and instruction-following using Vision-Language Models (VLMs), they remain limited in addressing the complexities of multimodal human-robot interactions (HRI). Motivated by this challenge, we introduce a lightweight language-to-vision feedback module that closes the loop between an LLM and the vision encoder in VLMs. The module projects image-token hidden states through a gated Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) back into the encoder input, prompting a second pass that reinterprets the scene under text context. We evaluate this approach on three robotics-centred tasks: navigation in a simulated environment (Habitat), sequential scene description (Mementos-Robotics), and human-intention recognition (our HRI dataset). Results show that our method improves Qwen 2.5 (7B) by $3.3\%$ (less distance), $+0.057$ description score, and $+2.93\%$ accuracy, with less than $3\%$ extra parameters; Gemma 3 (4B) and LLaVA OV 1.5 (4B) show mixed navigation results but gains $+0.111,+0.055$ and $+10.81\%,+4.79\%$ on the latter two tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/alessioGalatolo/VLM-Reasoning-for-Robotics

</details>


### [128] [RVN-Bench: A Benchmark for Reactive Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03953)
*Jaewon Lee,Jaeseok Heo,Gunmin Lee,Howoong Jun,Jeongwoo Oh,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RVN-Bench是一个用于室内移动机器人的碰撞感知视觉导航基准测试，基于Habitat 2.0模拟器构建，提供大规模多样的室内环境、碰撞感知导航任务定义、评估指标以及标准化训练和基准测试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往忽视碰撞问题或主要针对室外场景设计，不适合室内视觉导航需求。需要专门针对室内移动机器人的碰撞感知视觉导航基准测试。

Method: 基于Habitat 2.0模拟器和高保真HM3D场景构建，提供大规模多样的室内环境。定义碰撞感知导航任务：智能体在未见过的环境中仅使用视觉观察（无先验地图）到达顺序目标位置并避免碰撞。提供在线强化学习环境、轨迹图像数据集生成器以及捕获碰撞事件的负轨迹图像数据集工具。

Result: 在RVN-Bench上训练的策略能够有效泛化到未见过的环境，证明了其作为安全鲁棒视觉导航标准化基准的价值。

Conclusion: RVN-Bench是一个有价值的标准化基准测试，用于室内移动机器人的安全鲁棒视觉导航，填补了现有基准测试在碰撞感知和室内场景方面的空白。

Abstract: Safe visual navigation is critical for indoor mobile robots operating in cluttered environments. Existing benchmarks, however, often neglect collisions or are designed for outdoor scenarios, making them unsuitable for indoor visual navigation. To address this limitation, we introduce the reactive visual navigation benchmark (RVN-Bench), a collision-aware benchmark for indoor mobile robots. In RVN-Bench, an agent must reach sequential goal positions in previously unseen environments using only visual observations and no prior map, while avoiding collisions. Built on the Habitat 2.0 simulator and leveraging high-fidelity HM3D scenes, RVN-Bench provides large-scale, diverse indoor environments, defines a collision-aware navigation task and evaluation metrics, and offers tools for standardized training and benchmarking. RVN-Bench supports both online and offline learning by offering an environment for online reinforcement learning, a trajectory image dataset generator, and tools for producing negative trajectory image datasets that capture collision events. Experiments show that policies trained on RVN-Bench generalize effectively to unseen environments, demonstrating its value as a standardized benchmark for safe and robust visual navigation. Code and additional materials are available at: https://rvn-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [Structural Action Transformer for 3D Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03960)
*Xiaohan Lei,Min Wang,Bohong Weng,Wengang Zhou,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAT提出了一种基于结构中心视角的3D灵巧操作策略，将动作块重构为可变长度的无序关节轨迹序列，通过Transformer处理异构体，使用具身关节码本编码功能角色，实现跨体技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D观测和时间中心动作表示的方法难以捕捉3D空间关系，无法处理异构体差异，阻碍了从异构数据集通过模仿学习实现机器人灵巧操作的人类水平能力。

Method: 提出结构动作变换器(SAT)，将动作块重构为可变长度、无序的关节轨迹序列；引入具身关节码本编码关节功能角色和运动学特性；使用连续时间流匹配目标从3D点云生成轨迹；在大规模异构数据集上预训练，在仿真和真实任务上微调。

Result: 在所有基准测试中一致优于现有方法，展示了优越的样本效率和有效的跨体技能迁移能力。

Conclusion: 结构中心表示为高自由度异构机械臂的策略扩展提供了新路径，通过将动作视为结构实体而非时间序列，实现了更好的3D空间关系建模和跨体泛化能力。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in robots via imitation learning from heterogeneous datasets is hindered by the challenge of cross-embodiment skill transfer, particularly for high-DoF robotic hands. Existing methods, often relying on 2D observations and temporal-centric action representation, struggle to capture 3D spatial relations and fail to handle embodiment heterogeneity. This paper proposes the Structural Action Transformer (SAT), a new 3D dexterous manipulation policy that challenges this paradigm by introducing a structural-centric perspective. We reframe each action chunk not as a temporal sequence, but as a variable-length, unordered sequence of joint-wise trajectories. This structural formulation allows a Transformer to natively handle heterogeneous embodiments, treating the joint count as a variable sequence length. To encode structural priors and resolve ambiguity, we introduce an Embodied Joint Codebook that embeds each joint's functional role and kinematic properties. Our model learns to generate these trajectories from 3D point clouds via a continuous-time flow matching objective. We validate our approach by pre-training on large-scale heterogeneous datasets and fine-tuning on simulation and real-world dexterous manipulation tasks. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines, demonstrating superior sample efficiency and effective cross-embodiment skill transfer. This structural-centric representation offers a new path toward scaling policies for high-DoF, heterogeneous manipulators.

</details>


### [130] [Right in Time: Reactive Reasoning in Regulated Traffic Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03977)
*Simon Kohaut,Benedict Flade,Julian Eggert,Kristian Kersting,Devendra Singh Dhami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合概率一阶逻辑与反应式推理的在线精确推理框架，用于自动驾驶系统在共享交通空间中的实时安全合规决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概率一阶逻辑的自动驾驶决策方法计算成本高，通常只能用于飞行前检查，无法支持在线实时推理。需要开发能够处理不确定环境数据和逻辑交通规则的反应式推理框架

Method: 提出反应式任务设计框架，将概率任务设计（ProMis）与反应式电路（RC）结合，利用异构数据流的变化频率特性，将推理公式分解为可记忆化的独立任务，仅重新评估受新传感器数据影响的组件

Result: 在真实船舶数据和模拟城市密集无人机交通场景实验中，相比无反应式范式的ProMis，该方法实现了数量级的速度提升，使智能交通系统能够在运行中主动确保安全和法律合规

Conclusion: 该反应式推理框架实现了概率一阶逻辑的在线精确推理，使无人航空系统等智能交通系统能够实时处理不确定环境和逻辑规则，从依赖准备程序转向主动运行中合规

Abstract: Exact inference in probabilistic First-Order Logic offers a promising yet computationally costly approach for regulating the behavior of autonomous agents in shared traffic spaces. While prior methods have combined logical and probabilistic data into decision-making frameworks, their application is often limited to pre-flight checks due to the complexity of reasoning across vast numbers of possible universes. In this work, we propose a reactive mission design framework that jointly considers uncertain environmental data and declarative, logical traffic regulations. By synthesizing Probabilistic Mission Design (ProMis) with reactive reasoning facilitated by Reactive Circuits (RC), we enable online, exact probabilistic inference over hybrid domains. Our approach leverages the Frequency of Change inherent in heterogeneous data streams to subdivide inference formulas into memoized, isolated tasks, ensuring that only the specific components affected by new sensor data are re-evaluated. In experiments involving both real-world vessel data and simulated drone traffic in dense urban scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach provides orders of magnitude in speedup over ProMis without reactive paradigms. This allows intelligent transportation systems, such as Unmanned Aircraft Systems (UAS), to actively assert safety and legal compliance during operations rather than relying solely on preparation procedures.

</details>


### [131] [Map-Agnostic And Interactive Safety-Critical Scenario Generation via Multi-Objective Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03978)
*Wenyun Li,Zejian Deng,Chen Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于多目标蒙特卡洛树搜索的交通流级安全关键场景生成框架，通过统一评估函数平衡轨迹可行性与自然行为，实现现实且多样的碰撞事件生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以生成既真实又多样且具有明确交互逻辑的安全关键场景，需要一种能生成交通流级复杂碰撞事件的框架来验证自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性

Method: 采用多目标蒙特卡洛树搜索框架，将轨迹可行性和自然行为作为优化目标整合到统一评估函数中；引入混合UCB和LCB搜索策略平衡探索效率与风险规避；支持地图无关的交互式场景生成，每个车辆由SUMO微观交通模型驱动

Result: 在香港四个高风险事故区域的复杂城市环境中验证，框架达到85%的碰撞失败率，生成的轨迹在可行性和舒适度指标上表现优异；生成场景复杂度更高，表现为车辆里程和CO₂排放增加

Conclusion: 该工作为通过生成交通流级现实但罕见的极端案例来压力测试自动驾驶车辆提供了原则性解决方案，能够有效发现多样且真实的碰撞事件

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for validating the robustness of autonomous driving systems, yet existing methods often struggle to produce collisions that are both realistic and diverse while ensuring explicit interaction logic among traffic participants. This paper presents a novel framework for traffic-flow level safety-critical scenario generation via multi-objective Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). We reframe trajectory feasibility and naturalistic behavior as optimization objectives within a unified evaluation function, enabling the discovery of diverse collision events without compromising realism. A hybrid Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) and Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) search strategy is introduced to balance exploratory efficiency with risk-averse decision-making. Furthermore, our method is map-agnostic and supports interactive scenario generation with each vehicle individually powered by SUMO's microscopic traffic models, enabling realistic agent behaviors in arbitrary geographic locations imported from OpenStreetMap. We validate our approach across four high-risk accident zones in Hong Kong's complex urban environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework achieves an 85\% collision failure rate while generating trajectories with superior feasibility and comfort metrics. The resulting scenarios exhibit greater complexity, as evidenced by increased vehicle mileage and CO\(_2\) emissions. Our work provides a principled solution for stress testing autonomous vehicles through the generation of realistic yet infrequent corner cases at traffic-flow level.

</details>


### [132] [Force-Aware Residual DAgger via Trajectory Editing for Precision Insertion with Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04038)
*Yiou Huang,Ma Ning,Weichu Zhao,Zinuo Liu,Jun Sun,Qiufeng Wang,Yaran Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TER-DAgger：一种基于轨迹编辑残差数据集聚合的力感知人机协同模仿学习框架，用于接触密集型精密装配任务，通过优化轨迹编辑缓解协变量偏移，减少专家监控需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在接触密集型精密装配任务中潜力巨大，但实际部署面临协变量偏移和需要持续专家监控以从执行失败中恢复的问题。现有方法难以实现可扩展部署。

Method: 提出TER-DAgger框架：1）通过基于优化的轨迹编辑学习残差策略，平滑融合策略执行与人工修正轨迹；2）引入力感知失败预期机制，仅在预测与实测末端执行器力出现差异时触发人工干预；3）所有学习策略在笛卡尔阻抗控制框架内执行，确保接触交互中的顺应性和安全性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界精密装配任务中的广泛实验表明，TER-DAgger相比行为克隆、人工引导修正、重新训练和微调基线方法，平均成功率提升超过37%，有效缓解协变量偏移并实现可扩展部署。

Conclusion: TER-DAgger通过轨迹编辑残差学习和力感知失败预期机制，成功解决了模仿学习在接触密集型任务中的协变量偏移和持续专家监控问题，为精密装配任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) has shown strong potential for contact-rich precision insertion tasks. However, its practical deployment is often hindered by covariate shift and the need for continuous expert monitoring to recover from failures during execution. In this paper, we propose Trajectory Editing Residual Dataset Aggregation (TER-DAgger), a scalable and force-aware human-in-the-loop imitation learning framework that mitigates covariate shift by learning residual policies through optimization-based trajectory editing. This approach smoothly fuses policy rollouts with human corrective trajectories, providing consistent and stable supervision. Second, we introduce a force-aware failure anticipation mechanism that triggers human intervention only when discrepancies arise between predicted and measured end-effector forces, significantly reducing the requirement for continuous expert monitoring. Third, all learned policies are executed within a Cartesian impedance control framework, ensuring compliant and safe behavior during contact-rich interactions. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world precision insertion tasks show that TER-DAgger improves the average success rate by over 37\% compared to behavior cloning, human-guided correction, retraining, and fine-tuning baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in mitigating covariate shift and enabling scalable deployment in contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [133] [Sim2Sea: Sim-to-Real Policy Transfer for Maritime Vessel Navigation in Congested Waters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04057)
*Xinyu Cui,Xuanfa Jin,Xue Yan,Yongcheng Zeng,Luoyang Sun,Siying Wei,Ruizhi Zhang,Jian Zhao,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim2Sea是一个用于海上自主导航的仿真到现实框架，通过GPU加速并行仿真、双流时空策略和领域随机化，实现了在拥挤水域的零样本现实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在实际部署中存在显著的仿真到现实差距，主要源于仿真不精确、态势感知不足和不安全的探索策略。拥挤海上环境中的自主导航由于复杂的船舶交互和环境不确定性而具有挑战性。

Method: 1) GPU加速并行仿真器用于可扩展且精确的海上场景仿真；2) 双流时空策略处理复杂动力学和多模态感知，结合速度障碍引导的动作掩蔽机制确保安全高效探索；3) 针对性的领域随机化方案缩小仿真到现实差距。

Result: 仿真结果显示比现有基线方法收敛更快、轨迹更安全。纯仿真训练的策略能够零样本迁移到17吨无人船在真实世界拥挤水域中运行，验证了Sim2Sea的有效性。

Conclusion: Sim2Sea框架通过创新的仿真器设计、安全感知策略和领域随机化，成功实现了可靠的仿真到现实迁移，为实际自主海上导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in congested maritime environments is a critical capability for a wide range of real-world applications. However, it remains an unresolved challenge due to complex vessel interactions and significant environmental uncertainties. Existing methods often fail in practical deployment due to a substantial sim-to-real gap, which stems from imprecise simulation, inadequate situational awareness, and unsafe exploration strategies. To address these, we propose \textbf{Sim2Sea}, a comprehensive framework designed to bridge simulation and real-world execution. Sim2Sea advances in three key aspects. First, we develop a GPU-accelerated parallel simulator for scalable and accurate maritime scenario simulation. Second, we design a dual-stream spatiotemporal policy that handles complex dynamics and multi-modal perception, augmented with a velocity-obstacle-guided action masking mechanism to ensure safe and efficient exploration. Finally, a targeted domain randomization scheme helps bridge the sim-to-real gap. Simulation results demonstrate that our method achieves faster convergence and safer trajectories than established baselines. In addition, our policy trained purely in simulation successfully transfers zero-shot to a 17-ton unmanned vessel operating in real-world congested waters. These results validate the effectiveness of Sim2Sea in achieving reliable sim-to-real transfer for practical autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [134] [SaFeR: Safety-Critical Scenario Generation for Autonomous Driving Test via Feasibility-Constrained Token Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04071)
*Jinlong Cui,Fenghua Liang,Guo Yang,Chengcheng Tang,Jianxun Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaFeR：通过可行性约束的token重采样实现自动驾驶测试的安全关键场景生成，平衡对抗性、物理可行性和行为真实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶场景生成方法难以平衡三个冲突目标：对抗性关键性、物理可行性和行为真实性，需要一种能同时满足这些要求的新方法

Method: 将交通生成建模为离散的下一个token预测问题，使用Transformer作为真实性先验；提出差分注意力机制减少注意力噪声；基于Largest Feasible Region(LFR)的可行性约束，通过离线强化学习近似LFR，实施token重采样策略在可信区域内诱导对抗行为

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset和nuPlan上的闭环实验表明，SaFeR显著优于现有基线方法，实现更高的解决率和更好的运动学真实性，同时保持强大的对抗有效性

Conclusion: SaFeR通过可行性约束的token重采样方法，成功解决了自动驾驶场景生成中对抗性、可行性和真实性的平衡问题，为自动驾驶系统测试提供了有效的安全关键场景生成框架

Abstract: Safety-critical scenario generation is crucial for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing approaches often struggle to balance three conflicting objectives: adversarial criticality, physical feasibility, and behavioral realism. To bridge this gap, we propose SaFeR: safety-critical scenario generation for autonomous driving test via feasibility-constrained token resampling. We first formulate traffic generation as a discrete next token prediction problem, employing a Transformer-based model as a realism prior to capture naturalistic driving distributions. To capture complex interactions while effectively mitigating attention noise, we propose a novel differential attention mechanism within the realism prior. Building on this prior, SaFeR implements a novel resampling strategy that induces adversarial behaviors within a high-probability trust region to maintain naturalism, while enforcing a feasibility constraint derived from the Largest Feasible Region (LFR). By approximating the LFR via offline reinforcement learning, SaFeR effectively prevents the generation of theoretically inevitable collisions. Closed-loop experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset and nuPlan demonstrate that SaFeR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a higher solution rate and superior kinematic realism while maintaining strong adversarial effectiveness.

</details>


### [135] [Swimming Under Constraints: A Safe Reinforcement Learning Framework for Quadrupedal Bio-Inspired Propulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04073)
*Xinyu Cui,Fei Han,Hang Xu,Yongcheng Zeng,Luoyang Sun,Ruizhi Zhang,Jian Zhao,Haifeng Zhang,Weikun Li,Hao Chen,Jun Wang,Dixia Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACPPO-PID框架，通过PID调节的拉格朗日乘子、条件非对称裁剪和循环几何聚合，优化四足游泳的推进力同时最小化失稳波动，在流体环境中实现稳健的生物启发式运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发的水下推进虽然具有高推力和机动性优势，但容易受到升力波动等失稳力的影响，这些力在六自由度流体耦合中会被进一步放大。需要开发能够最大化前进推力同时最小化失稳波动的约束优化方法。

Method: 将四足游泳建模为约束优化问题，提出ACPPO-PID框架：1) 使用PID调节的拉格朗日乘子强制执行约束；2) 通过条件非对称裁剪加速学习；3) 通过循环几何聚合稳定更新。采用模仿学习初始化，并通过硬件拖曳水池实验进行精炼。

Result: ACPPO-PID生成的控制策略能有效转移到四足自由游泳实验中，相比最先进的基线方法，表现出改进的推力效率、减少的失稳力和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 约束感知的安全强化学习对于在复杂流体环境中实现稳健且可泛化的生物启发式运动至关重要，ACPPO-PID框架为此提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Bio-inspired aquatic propulsion offers high thrust and maneuverability but is prone to destabilizing forces such as lift fluctuations, which are further amplified by six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) fluid coupling. We formulate quadrupedal swimming as a constrained optimization problem that maximizes forward thrust while minimizing destabilizing fluctuations. Our proposed framework, Accelerated Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization with a PID-regulated Lagrange multiplier (ACPPO-PID), enforces constraints with a PID-regulated Lagrange multiplier, accelerates learning via conditional asymmetric clipping, and stabilizes updates through cycle-wise geometric aggregation. Initialized with imitation learning and refined through on-hardware towing-tank experiments, ACPPO-PID produces control policies that transfer effectively to quadrupedal free-swimming trials. Results demonstrate improved thrust efficiency, reduced destabilizing forces, and faster convergence compared with state-of-the-art baselines, underscoring the importance of constraint-aware safe RL for robust and generalizable bio-inspired locomotion in complex fluid environments.

</details>


### [136] [HBRB-BoW: A Retrained Bag-of-Words Vocabulary for ORB-SLAM via Hierarchical BRB-KMeans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04144)
*Minjae Lee,Sang-Min Choi,Gun-Woo Kim,Suwon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HBRB-BoW算法改进ORB-SLAM的视觉词典质量，通过分层聚类中引入全局实值流，在叶节点才进行二值化，提升词典的判别性和结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ORB-SLAM广泛使用的基于k-majority的BoW二值词典存在固有精度损失问题。传统二值聚类无法表示细微特征分布，导致视觉词退化，错误在分层树结构中累积传播，影响环境表示和位置识别能力。

Method: 提出分层二值-实值-返回(HBRB)-BoW算法，在分层聚类过程中集成全局实值流，保持高保真描述符信息直到叶节点才进行最终二值化，从而改进词典训练过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统方法相比，所提方法生成更具判别性和结构良好的词典，显著增强了复杂环境中视觉词典的表征完整性。替换ORB-SLAM默认词典文件预计能提升闭环检测和重定位任务性能。

Conclusion: HBRB-BoW通过改进分层二值词典训练算法，解决了传统方法中的精度损失和特征分布表示不足问题，为视觉SLAM系统提供了更高质量的视觉词典，有望提升ORB-SLAM在闭环检测和重定位方面的性能。

Abstract: In visual simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), the quality of the visual vocabulary is fundamental to the system's ability to represent environments and recognize locations. While ORB-SLAM is a widely used framework, its binary vocabulary, trained through the k-majority-based bag-of-words (BoW) approach, suffers from inherent precision loss. The inability of conventional binary clustering to represent subtle feature distributions leads to the degradation of visual words, a problem that is compounded as errors accumulate and propagate through the hierarchical tree structure. To address these structural deficiencies, this paper proposes hierarchical binary-to-real-and-back (HBRB)-BoW, a refined hierarchical binary vocabulary training algorithm. By integrating a global real-valued flow within the hierarchical clustering process, our method preserves high-fidelity descriptor information until the final binarization at the leaf nodes. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach yields a more discriminative and well-structured vocabulary than traditional methods, significantly enhancing the representational integrity of the visual dictionary in complex environments. Furthermore, replacing the default ORB-SLAM vocabulary file with our HBRB-BoW file is expected to improve performance in loop closing and relocalization tasks.

</details>


### [137] [GarmentPile++: Affordance-Driven Cluttered Garments Retrieval with Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04158)
*Mingleyang Li,Yuran Wang,Yue Chen,Tianxing Chen,Jiaqi Liang,Zishun Shen,Haoran Lu,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的衣物检索流水线，能够在衣物堆叠场景中根据语言指令安全、干净地检索衣物，并保证每次尝试只取出一件衣物，为下游任务提供可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有衣物操作研究大多假设初始状态只有单件衣物，而实际场景中衣物通常是堆叠的。需要解决在衣物堆中根据语言指令精确检索单件衣物的问题。

Method: 1) 融合视觉语言模型(VLM)的高层推理规划能力与视觉可供性感知的低层动作泛化能力；2) 使用SAM2进行衣物堆分割，为VLM推理提供充分视觉线索；3) 引入掩码微调机制处理初始分割不理想的情况；4) 部署双臂协作框架处理大型/长衣物及抓取点不当导致的过度下垂问题。

Result: 在真实世界和仿真环境中的多样化任务和场景中，该流水线均表现出持续有效性，能够可靠地执行衣物检索任务。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的衣物检索流水线成功解决了衣物堆叠场景中的单件衣物检索问题，通过视觉语言推理与视觉可供性感知的融合，以及双臂协作机制，为家庭助理机器人的衣物操作任务提供了稳健基础。

Abstract: Garment manipulation has attracted increasing attention due to its critical role in home-assistant robotics. However, the majority of existing garment manipulation works assume an initial state consisting of only one garment, while piled garments are far more common in real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel garment retrieval pipeline that can not only follow language instruction to execute safe and clean retrieval but also guarantee exactly one garment is retrieved per attempt, establishing a robust foundation for the execution of downstream tasks (e.g., folding, hanging, wearing). Our pipeline seamlessly integrates vision-language reasoning with visual affordance perception, fully leveraging the high-level reasoning and planning capabilities of VLMs alongside the generalization power of visual affordance for low-level actions. To enhance the VLM's comprehensive awareness of each garment's state within a garment pile, we employ visual segmentation model (SAM2) to execute object segmentation on the garment pile for aiding VLM-based reasoning with sufficient visual cues. A mask fine-tuning mechanism is further integrated to address scenarios where the initial segmentation results are suboptimal. In addition, a dual-arm cooperation framework is deployed to address cases involving large or long garments, as well as excessive garment sagging caused by incorrect grasping point determination, both of which are strenuous for a single arm to handle. The effectiveness of our pipeline are consistently demonstrated across diverse tasks and varying scenarios in both real-world and simulation environments. Project page: https://garmentpile2.github.io/.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Hip Exoskeleton Control Policy via Predictive Neuromusculoskeletal Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04166)
*Ilseung Park,Changseob Song,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼学习框架，通过仿真训练髋关节外骨骼控制策略，无需动作捕捉数据，并通过策略蒸馏部署到硬件上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统外骨骼控制器开发需要大量动作捕捉数据和生物力学标注，限制了在实验室环境外的可扩展性。需要一种更实用和可扩展的方法来减少实验负担。

Method: 提出了基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼学习框架：1）在仿真中训练强化学习教师策略，使用肌肉协同动作先验；2）采用两阶段课程学习，覆盖广泛的步行速度和坡度；3）通过策略蒸馏将仿真策略部署到硬件上。

Result: 仿真中：外骨骼辅助使平均肌肉激活降低达3.4%，平均正关节功率降低达7.0%，效果随步行速度增加而增强。硬件上：仿真学习的辅助模式在匹配的速度-坡度条件下得到保持（相关系数0.82，RMSE 0.03 Nm/kg），证明了无需额外硬件调优的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 基于物理的神经肌肉骨骼仿真可以作为外骨骼控制器开发的实用且可扩展的基础，显著减少设计阶段的实验负担。

Abstract: Developing exoskeleton controllers that generalize across diverse locomotor conditions typically requires extensive motion-capture data and biomechanical labeling, limiting scalability beyond instrumented laboratory settings. Here, we present a physics-based neuromusculoskeletal learning framework that trains a hip-exoskeleton control policy entirely in simulation, without motion-capture demonstrations, and deploys it on hardware via policy distillation. A reinforcement learning teacher policy is trained using a muscle-synergy action prior over a wide range of walking speeds and slopes through a two-stage curriculum, enabling direct comparison between assisted and no-exoskeleton conditions. In simulation, exoskeleton assistance reduces mean muscle activation by up to 3.4% and mean positive joint power by up to 7.0% on level ground and ramp ascent, with benefits increasing systematically with walking speed. On hardware, the assistance profiles learned in simulation are preserved across matched speed-slope conditions (r: 0.82, RMSE: 0.03 Nm/kg), providing quantitative evidence of sim-to-real transfer without additional hardware tuning. These results demonstrate that physics-based neuromusculoskeletal simulation can serve as a practical and scalable foundation for exoskeleton controller development, substantially reducing experimental burden during the design phase.

</details>


### [139] [GSeg3D: A High-Precision Grid-Based Algorithm for Safety-Critical Ground Segmentation in LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04208)
*Muhammad Haider Khan Lodhi,Christoph Hertzberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于点云数据的地面分割方法，旨在在自动驾驶和机器人安全关键场景中实现持续高精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在安全关键环境中往往达不到所需的高精度，导致误检测可能影响决策制定，而自动驾驶和机器人感知需要精确分离地面点与非地面点以确保安全可靠运行

Method: 提出一种地面分割方法（具体方法未在摘要中详细说明），旨在满足自动驾驶车辆和机器人系统在现实世界安全关键场景中的严格要求

Result: 该方法能够提供持续高精度的地面分割（具体量化结果未在摘要中提供）

Conclusion: 提出的地面分割方法能够满足自动驾驶和机器人系统在安全关键环境中的严格要求，通过提供持续高精度支持可靠的感知和决策制定

Abstract: Ground segmentation in point cloud data is the process of separating ground points from non-ground points. This task is fundamental for perception in autonomous driving and robotics, where safety and reliable operation depend on the precise detection of obstacles and navigable surfaces. Existing methods often fall short of the high precision required in safety-critical environments, leading to false detections that can compromise decision-making. In this work, we present a ground segmentation approach designed to deliver consistently high precision, supporting the stringent requirements of autonomous vehicles and robotic systems operating in real-world, safety-critical scenarios.

</details>


### [140] [PRAM-R: A Perception-Reasoning-Action-Memory Framework with LLM-Guided Modality Routing for Adaptive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04222)
*Yi Zhang,Xian Zhang,Saisi Zhao,Yinglei Song,Chengdong Wu,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRAM-R：基于LLM引导模态路由的自适应自动驾驶框架，通过双循环异步设计和分层记忆模块实现高效多模态感知，在保持性能的同时显著减少计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知虽然能提升自动驾驶的鲁棒性，但所有传感器持续工作会产生不必要的计算成本。需要一种能够自适应选择传感器模态的智能系统，在保证性能的同时优化计算效率。

Method: 提出PRAM-R框架，采用异步双循环设计：快速反应循环处理感知和控制，慢速审慎循环进行推理驱动的模态选择和记忆更新。LLM路由器根据环境上下文和传感器诊断选择并加权模态，分层记忆模块保持时间一致性并支持长期适应。

Result: 合成压力测试显示通过基于滞后的稳定化减少了87.2%的路由振荡。在nuScenes数据集上的真实世界验证表明，在复杂城市场景中，模态减少6.22%，记忆召回率20%，同时保持与全模态基线相当的轨迹精度。

Conclusion: LLM增强架构配合分层记忆能够实现高效、自适应的多模态感知，在自动驾驶中显著降低计算成本而不牺牲性能，为资源受限的自动驾驶系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal perception enables robust autonomous driving but incurs unnecessary computational cost when all sensors remain active. This paper presents PRAM-R, a unified Perception-Reasoning-Action-Memory framework with LLM-Guided Modality Routing for adaptive autonomous driving. PRAM-R adopts an asynchronous dual-loop design: a fast reactive loop for perception and control, and a slow deliberative loop for reasoning-driven modality selection and memory updates. An LLM router selects and weights modalities using environmental context and sensor diagnostics, while a hierarchical memory module preserves temporal consistency and supports long-term adaptation. We conduct a two-stage evaluation: (1) synthetic stress tests for stability analysis and (2) real-world validation on the nuScenes dataset. Synthetic stress tests confirm 87.2% reduction in routing oscillations via hysteresis-based stabilization. Real-world validation on nuScenes shows 6.22% modality reduction with 20% memory recall while maintaining comparable trajectory accuracy to full-modality baselines in complex urban scenarios. Our work demonstrates that LLM-augmented architectures with hierarchical memory achieve efficient, adaptive multimodal perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [141] [AMP2026: A Multi-Platform Marine Robotics Dataset for Tracking and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04225)
*Edwin Meriaux,Shuo Wen,David Widhalm,Zhizun Wang,Junming Shi,Mariana Sosa Guzmán,Kalvik Jakkala,Bennett Carley,Elias Sokolova,Yogesh Girdhar,Monika Roznere,Jason O'Kane,Junaed Sattar,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AMP2026是一个多平台海洋机器人数据集，包含空中、水面和水下同步数据，用于支持多视角跟踪和海洋环境地图构建研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋环境因动态表面、有限能见度以及空中、水面和水下传感模态之间的复杂交互，对感知和自主性提出了重大挑战。需要公开可用的多平台数据集来推动海洋感知和多机器人观测研究。

Method: 通过多次实地部署收集多平台海洋机器人数据，包括空中无人机、船载相机和潜航机器人平台的同步数据，以及相关的定位和遥测信息。详细描述了数据收集方法、传感器配置和数据集组织。

Result: 创建了AMP2026数据集，这是一个公开可用的多平台海洋机器人数据集，包含同步的空中、水面和水下传感数据，支持多视角跟踪和海洋环境地图构建等研究任务。

Conclusion: AMP2026数据集为海洋感知和多机器人观测研究提供了有价值的资源，通过多平台同步数据支持复杂海洋环境下的感知算法开发和验证。

Abstract: Marine environments present significant challenges for perception and autonomy due to dynamic surfaces, limited visibility, and complex interactions between aerial, surface, and submerged sensing modalities. This paper introduces the Aerial Marine Perception Dataset (AMP2026), a multi-platform marine robotics dataset collected across multiple field deployments designed to support research in two primary areas: multi-view tracking and marine environment mapping. The dataset includes synchronized data from aerial drones, boat-mounted cameras, and submerged robotic platforms, along with associated localization and telemetry information. The goal of this work is to provide a publicly available dataset enabling research in marine perception and multi-robot observation scenarios. This paper describes the data collection methodology, sensor configurations, dataset organization, and intended research tasks supported by the dataset.

</details>


### [142] [RoboLight: A Dataset with Linearly Composable Illumination for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04249)
*Shutong Jin,Jin Yang,Muhammad Zahid,Florian T. Pokorny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboLight是首个在系统变化光照条件下采集同步机器人操作数据的真实世界数据集，包含真实采集和合成扩展两部分，支持光照变化研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作数据集缺乏对光照条件变化的系统性控制，而光照变化是机器人感知和操作中的关键挑战。需要能够系统研究光照变化对机器人性能影响的数据集。

Method: 1. 构建Light Cube系统：配备8个可编程RGB LED灯，可独立控制颜色、方向和强度三个维度；2. 采集RoboLight-Real：2800个真实世界片段，使用HDR格式记录；3. 生成RoboLight-Synthetic：利用光传输的线性特性，在HDR图像空间进行插值，生成196,000个合成片段；4. 设计专门任务：针对不同光照维度设计相应任务，使用不同几何形状和材质的物体。

Result: 1. 创建了首个系统控制光照变化的机器人操作数据集；2. 通过定性分析和真实世界策略部署验证了数据集质量；3. 展示了三个代表性用例；4. 数据集、系统软件和硬件设计将开源发布。

Conclusion: RoboLight填补了机器人操作数据集中光照变化控制的空白，通过真实采集和合成扩展相结合的方法，为研究光照变化对机器人感知和操作的影响提供了重要资源，支持相关领域持续研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce RoboLight, the first real-world robotic manipulation dataset capturing synchronized episodes under systematically varied lighting conditions. RoboLight consists of two components. (a) RoboLight-Real contains 2,800 real-world episodes collected in our custom Light Cube setup, a calibrated system equipped with eight programmable RGB LED lights. It includes structured illumination variation along three independently controlled dimensions: color, direction, and intensity. Each dimension is paired with a dedicated task featuring objects of diverse geometries and materials to induce perceptual challenges. All image data are recorded in high-dynamic-range (HDR) format to preserve radiometric accuracy. Leveraging the linearity of light transport, we introduce (b) RoboLight-Synthetic, comprising 196,000 episodes synthesized through interpolation in the HDR image space of RoboLight-Real. In principle, RoboLight-Synthetic can be arbitrarily expanded by refining the interpolation granularity. We further verify the dataset quality through qualitative analysis and real-world policy roll-outs, analyzing task difficulty, distributional diversity, and the effectiveness of synthesized data. We additionally demonstrate three representative use cases of the proposed dataset. The full dataset, along with the system software and hardware design, will be released as open-source to support continued research.

</details>


### [143] [OmniPlanner: Universal Exploration and Inspection Path Planning across Robot Morphologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04284)
*Angelos Zacharia,Mihir Dharmadhikari,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniPlanner：一个用于空中、地面和水下机器人的统一规划框架，通过平台抽象层实现跨领域泛化，在多种复杂环境中验证了其探索和检查效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划方法大多局限于特定领域（空中、地面或水下），缺乏跨平台的可扩展性和通用性，限制了自主机器人在复杂非结构化环境中的广泛应用

Method: 提出OmniPlanner统一规划框架，集成体积探索、基于视点的检查和目标到达行为，采用模块化架构和平台抽象层来捕获不同形态机器人的传感、可通行性和运动约束

Result: 在模拟研究和实地部署中验证了框架的有效性，包括地下矿井、工业设施、森林、潜艇掩体和结构化室外环境，相比现有基准方法表现出更强的鲁棒性、跨领域泛化能力和探索检查效率

Conclusion: OmniPlanner为跨领域机器人规划提供了统一的解决方案，通过平台抽象实现了最小化重新调优的跨领域泛化，在多样化场景中展现出优越性能

Abstract: Autonomous robotic systems are increasingly deployed for mapping, monitoring, and inspection in complex and unstructured environments. However, most existing path planning approaches remain domain-specific (i.e., either on air, land, or sea), limiting their scalability and cross-platform applicability. This article presents OmniPlanner, a unified planning framework for autonomous exploration and inspection across aerial, ground, and underwater robots. The method integrates volumetric exploration and viewpoint-based inspection, alongside target reach behaviors within a single modular architecture, complemented by a platform abstraction layer that captures morphology-specific sensing, traversability and motion constraints. This enables the same planning strategy to generalize across distinct mobility domains with minimal retuning. The framework is validated through extensive simulation studies and field deployments in underground mines, industrial facilities, forests, submarine bunkers, and structured outdoor environments. Across these diverse scenarios, OmniPlanner demonstrates robust performance, consistent cross-domain generalization, and improved exploration and inspection efficiency compared to representative state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Compliant In-hand Rolling Manipulation Using Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04301)
*Huan Weng,Yifei Chen,Kevin M. Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究多指机器人手的掌内滚动操作，利用配备触觉指尖的柔性手指实现接触位置和力矩信息反馈，开发了柔性准静态滚动操作的运动方程和控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多指机器人手的掌内滚动操作问题，旨在利用柔性手指和触觉传感器实现精确的对象姿态控制，解决传统刚性抓取在精细操作中的局限性。

Method: 推导了柔性准静态掌内滚动操作的运动方程，设计了基于多指触觉反馈的指尖滚动操作控制器，该控制器利用接触位置和力矩信息实现期望的对象扭转控制。

Result: 在仿真中验证了接触力学模型，并在实验机器人系统上测试了控制器的有效性，展示了多指协同滚动操作的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的柔性多指滚动操作框架能够有效实现掌内对象的精确姿态控制，为机器人精细操作任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate in-hand rolling manipulation using a multifingered robot hand, where each finger is compliant and equipped with a tactile fingertip providing contact location and wrench information. We derive the equations of motion for compliant quasistatic in-hand rolling manipulation and formulate a fingertip rolling manipulation controller for multiple fingers to achieve a desired object twist within a grasp. The contact mechanics are demonstrated in simulation and the controller is tested on an experimental robot system.

</details>


### [145] [Perception-Aware Time-Optimal Planning for Quadrotor Waypoint Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04305)
*Chao Qin,Jiaxu Xing,Rudolf Reiter,Angel Romero,Yifan Lin,Hugh H. -T. Liu,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的时间最优轨迹优化框架，将感知约束与非线性动力学、执行器限制、空气动力学、相机视野约束和几何门表示相结合，实现视觉四旋翼的高速自主飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间最优轨迹规划方法通常忽略车辆动力学、环境几何和机载状态估计视觉要求之间的紧密耦合，导致动态可行的轨迹可能因视觉质量下降而在闭环执行中失败。

Method: 提出统一的时间最优轨迹优化框架，结合信息论位置不确定性度量来量化视觉状态估计质量，通过三个感知目标集成到规划器中：位置不确定性最小化、顺序视野约束和前瞻对齐。开发模型预测轮廓跟踪控制器来精确跟踪感知感知轨迹。

Result: 实验显示真实世界飞行速度达9.8 m/s，平均跟踪误差0.07 m，在具有挑战性的Split-S课程上闭环成功率从55%提高到100%。

Conclusion: 该系统为研究感知感知、时间最优自主飞行的基本极限提供了可扩展的基准，实现了速度与感知可靠性之间的系统权衡探索。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor flight pushes the limits of control, actuation, and onboard perception. While time-optimal trajectory planning has been extensively studied, existing approaches typically neglect the tight coupling between vehicle dynamics, environmental geometry, and the visual requirements of onboard state estimation. As a result, trajectories that are dynamically feasible may fail in closed-loop execution due to degraded visual quality. This paper introduces a unified time-optimal trajectory optimization framework for vision-based quadrotors that explicitly incorporates perception constraints alongside full nonlinear dynamics, rotor actuation limits, aerodynamic effects, camera field-of-view constraints, and convex geometric gate representations.
  The proposed formulation solves minimum-time lap trajectories for arbitrary racetracks with diverse gate shapes and orientations, while remaining numerically robust and computationally efficient. We derive an information-theoretic position uncertainty metric to quantify visual state-estimation quality and integrate it into the planner through three perception objectives: position uncertainty minimization, sequential field-of-view constraints, and look-ahead alignment. This enables systematic exploration of the trade-offs between speed and perceptual reliability.
  To accurately track the resulting perception-aware trajectories, we develop a model predictive contouring tracking controller that separates lateral and progress errors. Experiments demonstrate real-world flight speeds up to 9.8 m/s with 0.07 m average tracking error, and closed-loop success rates improved from 55% to 100% on a challenging Split-S course. The proposed system provides a scalable benchmark for studying the fundamental limits of perception-aware, time-optimal autonomous flight.

</details>


### [146] [Gaussian Mixture-Based Inverse Perception Contract for Uncertainty-Aware Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04329)
*Bingyao Du,Joonkyung Kim,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GM-IPC方法，使用高斯混合模型表示感知不确定性，通过多模态椭圆置信集提高机器人导航性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPC方法使用单一椭圆集表示不确定性，无法捕捉细粒度感知误差的多模态和非规则结构，导致过度保守的集合和导航性能下降

Method: 引入基于高斯混合模型的逆感知契约(GM-IPC)，使用高斯混合模型导出的椭圆置信集并集来表示不确定性，提出学习框架训练GM-IPC以考虑概率包含、分布匹配和空空间惩罚

Result: GM-IPC能够捕捉细粒度、多模态和非凸的误差结构，提供形式化保证，产生的集合既有效又紧凑，可用于下游规划框架实现实时安全导航

Conclusion: GM-IPC扩展了IPC表示能力，能够捕捉复杂感知误差结构，在保持概率安全的同时实现更少保守、更自适应的机器人运动

Abstract: Reliable navigation in cluttered environments requires perception outputs that are not only accurate but also equipped with uncertainty sets suitable for safe control. An inverse perception contract (IPC) provides such a connection by mapping perceptual estimates to sets that contain the ground truth with high confidence. Existing IPC formulations, however, instantiate uncertainty as a single ellipsoidal set and rely on deterministic trust scores to guide robot motion. Such a representation cannot capture the multi-modal and irregular structure of fine-grained perception errors, often resulting in over-conservative sets and degraded navigation performance. In this work, we introduce Gaussian Mixture-based Inverse Perception Contract (GM-IPC), which extends IPC to represent uncertainty with unions of ellipsoidal confidence sets derived from Gaussian mixture models. This design moves beyond deterministic single-set abstractions, enabling fine-grained, multi-modal, and non-convex error structures to be captured with formal guarantees. A learning framework is presented that trains GM-IPC to account for probabilistic inclusion, distribution matching, and empty-space penalties, ensuring both validity and compactness of the predicted sets. We further show that the resulting uncertainty characterizations can be leveraged in downstream planning frameworks for real-time safe navigation, enabling less conservative and more adaptive robot motion while preserving safety in a probabilistic manner.

</details>


### [147] [Tendon Force Modeling for Sim2Real Transfer of Reinforcement Learning Policies for Tendon-Driven Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04351)
*Valentin Yuryev,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种建模肌腱驱动系统伺服电机肌腱力的方法，通过数据驱动技术结合上下文历史和新型测试台，减少RL策略从仿真到实物的转移差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动机器人具有灵活性和顺应性优势，但复杂肌腱系统的控制具有挑战性。基于仿真的强化学习方法受限于仿真到实物的差距，特别是执行器和系统动力学导致的肌腱力预测不准确，限制了RL策略在真实机器人上的转移效果

Method: 1) 开发数据驱动的肌腱力建模方法，利用上下文历史和新型测试台收集接触丰富的交互数据；2) 采用基于Transformer的模型预测肌腱力；3) 将学习到的力模型集成到GPU加速的肌腱力驱动刚体仿真中训练RL控制器

Result: 1) Transformer模型能够预测肌腱力，误差在最大电机力的3%以内，且具有机器人无关性；2) 集成学习模型后，测试轨迹的仿真到实物差距减少41%；3) 使用该模型训练的RL控制器在真实肌腱驱动机器人手指指尖姿态跟踪任务中性能提升50%

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效减少肌腱驱动系统的仿真到实物差距，使RL策略更广泛地应用于肌腱系统，提升灵巧操作器和软体机器人的能力。该方法具有通用性，可适用于不同的执行器和机器人系统

Abstract: Robots which make use of soft or compliant inter- actions often leverage tendon-driven actuation which enables actuators to be placed more flexibly, and compliance to be maintained. However, controlling complex tendon systems is challenging. Simulation paired with reinforcement learning (RL) could be enable more complex behaviors to be generated. Such methods rely on torque and force-based simulation roll- outs which are limited by the sim-to-real gap, stemming from the actuator and system dynamics, resulting in poor transfer of RL policies onto real robots. To address this, we propose a method to model the tendon forces produced by typical servo motors, focusing specifically on the transfer of RL policies for a tendon driven finger. Our approach extends existing data- driven techniques by leveraging contextual history and a novel data collection test-bench. This test-bench allows us to capture tendon forces undergo contact-rich interactions typical of real- world manipulation. We then utilize our force estimation model in a GPU-accelerated tendon force-driven rigid body simulation to train RL-based controllers. Our transformer-based model is capable of predicting tendon forces within 3% of the maximum motor force and is robot-agnostic. By integrating our learned model into simulation, we reduce the sim-to-real gap for test trajectories by 41%. RL-based controller trained with our model achieves a 50% improvement in fingertip pose tracking tasks on real tendon-driven robotic fingers. This approach is generalizable to different actuators and robot systems, and can enable RL policies to be used widely across tendon systems, advancing capabilities of dexterous manipulators and soft robots.

</details>


### [148] [A Soft Robotic Demonstration in the Stratospher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04352)
*Codrin Tugui,Tirth Thakar,Anatol Gogoj,Alexander White,Ang Leo Li,Alexander Yin,Edward Pomianek,Mihai Duduta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种紫外光交联硅弹性体新方法，用于制造能在极端环境（-40°C至120°C、近真空）下工作的介电弹性体驱动器，并通过平流层气球任务验证了其在太空类似条件下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太空及其他极端环境中的机器需要兼具韧性和适应性。软机器人虽具适应性优势，但多数无法承受平流层的极端压力和温度。介电弹性体驱动器作为固态柔性电容器能克服部分限制，但弹性体的韧性限制了其工作窗口。

Method: 采用三甲基(甲基环戊二烯基)铂(IV)作为催化剂，在紫外光下通过氢硅烷与乙烯基反应交联硅弹性体。这种碳-碳键形成机制实现了快速紫外光加工，并赋予介电弹性体驱动器优异的机电性能。

Result: 新材料在-40°C和120°C以及近真空条件下的控制实验中表现出优于现有丙烯酸和硅化学材料的韧性优势。将采用该新型硅材料制作的夹持器集成到高空气球测试载荷中，两次平流层气球任务（最高23.6公里海拔、<0.05大气压、-55°C）验证了介电弹性体驱动器在太空类似条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 紫外光交联硅弹性体技术显著提升了介电弹性体驱动器在极端环境下的性能，证明了其作为太空软机器人技术的可行性。该化学构建模块和催化剂组合还可进一步扩展，以解决硅材料的其他挑战，包括粘附和增材制造。

Abstract: Machines designed for operation in Space, as well as other extreme environments, need to be both resilient and adaptable when mission parameters change. Soft robots offer advantages in adaptability, but most lack resilience to the pressure and temperature extremes found as close as the Stratosphere. Dielectric elastomer actuators overcome some of those limitations when built as solid state compliant capacitors capable of converting electrical energy into mechanical work, but the elastomer resilience limits the device's operating window. Here we present a crosslinking mechanism for silicone elastomers under ultraviolet light using trimethyl(methylcyclopentadienyl)platinum(IV) as a catalyst to react hydrosilane to vinyl groups. The formation of carbon-carbon bonds enables fast processing under UV light and exceptional electro-mechanical performance in dielectric elastomer actuators. The material resilience advantage is demonstrated in controlled experiments at -40° and 120° C, as well as near vacuum, in comparison with state-of-the-art acrylic and silicone chemistries. Fully autonomous systems controlling grippers made with the novel silicone were integrated into payloads for high altitude balloon testing. Two stratospheric balloon missions were carried out and demonstrated DEAs as a viable soft robotic technology under space-like conditions (as high as 23.6 km elevation, at <0.05 atm and -55° C). The combinations of chemical building blocks and catalyst can be further expanded to address other challenges for silicones, including adhesion and additive manufacturing.

</details>


### [149] [RoboCasa365: A Large-Scale Simulation Framework for Training and Benchmarking Generalist Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04356)
*Soroush Nasiriany,Sepehr Nasiriany,Abhiram Maddukuri,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCasa365是一个全面的家庭移动操作模拟基准，包含365个日常任务、2500个多样化厨房环境、600+小时人类演示数据和1600+小时合成演示数据，用于系统评估通用机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习领域缺乏可复现、大规模的系统评估基准来衡量通用机器人实现程度，需要填补这一空白以推动通用机器人发展。

Method: 基于RoboCasa平台构建RoboCasa365基准，包含365个日常任务、2500个多样化厨房环境，收集600+小时人类演示数据和1600+小时合成演示数据，支持多任务学习、机器人基础模型训练和终身学习等不同问题设置的系统评估。
